0857 — wheel rollerout

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0857 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: kneeling.

An ab wheel rollout from the knees, rolling forward and back with control. It builds a strong core and abs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Kneel on the floor and place the wheel roller in front of you. Place your hands on the handles of the wheel roller and extend your arms straight out in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0857.json#/instructions/0, 0857.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Place your hands on the handles of the wheel roller and extend your arms straight out in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0857.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Settle the documented knee or knees on their support, with comfortable lower-leg placement; do not invent machine pad spacing or a missing half-kneeling side.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 0857.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Ab wheel. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0857.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Kneel on the floor and place the wheel roller in front of you. 0857.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your hands on the handles of the wheel roller and extend your arms straight out in front of you. 0857.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core muscles and slowly roll the wheel forward, keeping your back straight and your abs tight. 0857.json#/instructions/2
  4. Continue rolling forward until your body is fully extended and your arms are overhead. 0857.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment, then slowly roll the wheel back towards your knees, maintaining control and keeping your abs engaged. 0857.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0857.json#/instructions/5

Short, all-out runs followed by rest. They build speed, stamina and a strong cardio base.

Proposed staging

0859 — wrist rollerer

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0859 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: prone.

A wrist roller that winds a weight up on a rope or bar by turning the wrists. It builds the forearms and grip.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach a weight to one end of a rope or bar. Hold the other end of the rope or bar with both hands, palms facing down. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms fully extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0859.json#/instructions/0, 0859.json#/instructions/1, 0859.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Hold the other end of the rope or bar with both hands, palms facing down. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms fully extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0859.json#/instructions/1, 0859.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms fully extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0859.json#/instructions/2

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0859.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach a weight to one end of a rope or bar. 0859.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the other end of the rope or bar with both hands, palms facing down. 0859.json#/instructions/1
  3. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms fully extended in front of you. 0859.json#/instructions/2
  4. Slowly roll the weight up towards your hands by flexing your wrists. 0859.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the weight back down to the starting position. 0859.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0859.json#/instructions/5

0860 — cable kickback

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0860 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A cable triceps kickback, hinging forward and extending the forearm back. It isolates the triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand facing a cable machine with your feet shoulder-width apart. Hold the cable handle with your right hand and step back to create tension in the cable.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0860.json#/instructions/0, 0860.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the cable handle with your right hand and step back to create tension in the cable.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0860.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand facing a cable machine with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0860.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0860.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand facing a cable machine with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0860.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the cable handle with your right hand and step back to create tension in the cable. 0860.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 0860.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your upper arm close to your body and your elbow bent at a 90-degree angle. 0860.json#/instructions/3
  5. Extend your forearm backward, straightening your arm fully. 0860.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause for a moment, then slowly return to the starting position. 0860.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch sides. 0860.json#/instructions/6

0861 — cable seated row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0861 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated cable row, pulling the handles toward the torso. It trains the back and biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the cable row machine with your feet flat on the footrests and your knees slightly bent. Grasp the handles with an overhand grip, keeping your back straight and your shoulders relaxed.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0861.json#/instructions/0, 0861.json#/instructions/1

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0861.json#/instructions/1

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0861.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the cable row machine with your feet flat on the footrests and your knees slightly bent. 0861.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grasp the handles with an overhand grip, keeping your back straight and your shoulders relaxed. 0861.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pull the handles towards your body, squeezing your shoulder blades together. 0861.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the peak of the movement, then slowly release the handles back to the starting position. 0861.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0861.json#/instructions/4

0862 — cable twist (up-down)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0862 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A cable twist that rotates the torso with a handle held at the chest. It trains the obliques and abs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, facing the cable machine. Hold the cable handle with both hands in front of your chest, keeping your arms slightly bent.

  2. Hold the cable handle with both hands in front of your chest, keeping your arms slightly bent. 0862.json#/instructions/1

  4. Pause for a moment at the end of the rotation, then slowly return to the starting position. 0862.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat the rotation to the opposite side. 0862.json#/instructions/4
  6. Continue alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 0862.json#/instructions/5

0863 — dumbbell lying external shoulder rotation

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0863 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: side_lying.

A side-lying rotation with a light dumbbell, turning the forearm upward with the elbow at the side. It strengthens the rotator cuff.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your side on a flat bench with your upper arm against your side and your elbow bent 90 degrees. Hold a dumbbell in your hand with your palm facing down.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0863.json#/instructions/0, 0863.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Lie on your side on a flat bench with your upper arm against your side and your elbow bent 90 degrees. Hold a dumbbell in your hand with your palm facing down.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0863.json#/instructions/0, 0863.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stack the legs comfortably, with a slight bend in the lower support leg if needed for balance; preserve the source's working-leg motion.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 0863.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0863.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your side on a flat bench with your upper arm against your side and your elbow bent 90 degrees. 0863.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold a dumbbell in your hand with your palm facing down. 0863.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your upper arm against your side, slowly rotate your forearm upward as far as possible. 0863.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your forearm back down to the starting position. 0863.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch sides. 0863.json#/instructions/4

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A single-leg squat holding a dumbbell. It builds leg strength and balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold a dumbbell at your chest or by your side. Stand on one leg with the other lifted in front. Sit your hips back and lower under control.

A sideways step up under the smith bar. It works the quads and glutes one leg at a time.

Proposed staging

Pull a band up along your body toward chest height. It works the shoulders and upper traps with gentle, joint-friendly resistance.
